# Current milestone

As of 27 Oct 2026: M0–M6 software is integrated on main (M5 has its bounded gate report) and M7 final integration is open. The owner reviewed 105 screens on 26 Oct (51 «موافق», 54 change requests) → rows OR-01…OR-32 in «Owner review page — 26 Oct» below, the current queue.
Owner-iPhone acceptance is NOT done yet (device signing needs the paid Apple Developer account); everything is verified on the simulator only. M7 software already has guarded prayer/letter/travel Activities, the availability-gated iOS 27 widget, title-only opt-in Spotlight, privacy manifests, local PDF OCR/search, Zakat sources and store/privacy text in Madar/Distribution; direct @als stays optional and deferred.
M7 is NOT complete: manual source/render trace, owner-device/system and iOS 27 runtime proof, resource release evidence, real-device performance and signed distribution/public store details remain.
iCloud: the owner chose CloudKit (24 Oct); the code is written and gated (queue 29), and the default build stays local-only until the paid account signs a CloudKit build. Preserve RA2, RP1, RU1 and existing records.

# How to use this board

- Take the first `ready` row. «Owner review page — 26 Oct» (OR-01…OR-32) is the current queue and comes first; «Next work» is the older numbered queue (list items, not table rows): go down it for anything still `in progress` or `ready`.
- Status values and the visual-acceptance rule are under «# Board»; only the owner sets `approved`. Owner decisions and conflicts: `docs/DECISIONS.md`; open questions: «Waiting on the owner» and «Ideas».
- Finished sections (every row approved, done or deferred) live in `docs/archive/PROGRESS-2026-10-27-finished-sections.md`; the index «Finished sections (archived 27 Oct)» below lists their card IDs.
- Older «Last checked» lines are in `docs/archive/progress-last-checked.md`; older handovers in `docs/archive/handovers-2026-10-26.md`.
- One handover per session under «# Handovers»; keep the newest three and move older ones to `docs/archive/`.

# Board

Status values: `ready` · `in progress` · `blocked: <reason>` · `matched — awaiting owner` · `approved` (owner only) · `done` (engines and flows without a screen).

Every screen card follows protocol §2: canonical files → default-size screenshots → `python3 Madar/scripts/compare-screens.py <board> <shots>` → fix → `matched — awaiting owner` (default text size only; no enlarged-text testing, owner 13 October 2026). Spec for every prayer card: §7.2 `docs/HANDOFF/sections/09-r-salah.md` (the part for that screen), §5 shapes `05-ds.md`, and the fixes listed.

| OR-32 | Mihbara drawer (M6-V15) | the drawer is useless | owner picked mockup A (26 Oct): a large «أكمل من حيث وقفت» card for the last-read book (cover, title, page N of M, percent) with one «أكمل القراءة» button opening the saved locator, then the library as a cover grid; replaces the drawer; keep Mihbara's look | matched — awaiting owner (Claude 27 Oct): the Sunnah drawer under the stand is gone. In its place «أكمل من حيث وقفت»: the cover of the last book you were reading that is not already on the stand, its title, «صفحة N من M», percent with a gold bar, and «أكمل القراءة» (EPUB/PDF open at the saved locator; a paper book opens its card at its page). With only one book in hand the stand already continues it, so no card shows. «مكتبتي» is now a wall of covers, three to a row, per shelf section, with shelf, percent and size under each; search, shelf filter, long-press menu and identifiers kept. The day's hadith stays on the wird strip under the stand. **Verified:** continue card → book → cover library 1/1 (`or32b.xcresult`); only-one-book no card, library counts, long-press to finished 3/3 (`or32a.xcresult`); shots `M6-V15-continue{,-opened}-L`, `M6-V15-library-covers-L`. **Not verified:** EPUB continue from the card (same `openBook` route as the stand), device. |

| AU-57 | Paying a card beyond all it owes keeps «لك فعلًا» (Claude, 29 Oct) | Haiku hunt of the money engine: a card payment larger than the statement plus the unbilled was clamped on the debt side only, so «لك فعلًا» fell by the extra although F01 makes a payment a transfer. `CreditCardState.creditMinor` keeps the extra; the basis counts it with the cash (your money at the bank), «عليك» never goes below 0, and the basis screen lists «رصيد دائن، <البطاقة>» with one sentence. Review (money rule, one pass, Sonnet): the first design (negative debt) made «عليك» negative and stopped zakat; both Highs fixed by moving credit to the cash side. Kept on purpose: a refund larger than the card's debt stays clamped in `cardDebts`, because a bank statement may already net the refund and crediting it again would overstate «لك فعلًا» (second review pass, 29 Oct). Left for later: card reconcile still expects 0 for a card in credit; BNPL overpayment is still clamped. ## Waiting on the owner

**Owner rule (18 Oct): «اي شي ينتظر قراري جمعهن كلهن اخر شي لكن مهب شي يوقف عليه التطوير».** A pending owner decision never stops development. Do the safe default, or skip that one item and take the next ready card. Add the question to this list, and the owner answers them all together at the end.

Owner, 22 Oct: the device run (paid developer account) and the review of built work wait a few days. Meanwhile the owner sends their current real data from the old Madar app for a trial import; problems are expected and fixed from it. Agent handling: work only on a copy, import into a separate simulator test store, never into the owner's real store and never off the Mac; record every mismatch as a board row.

Owner, 22 Oct (trial findings): the owner never typed expenses by hand. The 402 older money rows (3 Jul to 21 Sep, Latin merchant notes, no time, no message text) came from an earlier automatic source; bank-message rows with text start on 21 Sep. Import them as automatic rows without message text, never as «يدوي». Diary photos are only references in the backup; the owner thinks they are right that the old app kept no copies. Open question, not blocking: what fed the old app before 21 Sep (Apple Pay automation?).

Owner, 23 Oct: (1) Habits do not feel simple. He wants much more flexibility, in the habits themselves and in how they are shown, and a room that is far easier, clearer and simpler. Unclear visual wish: show 2–3 mockups first, the owner picks, then build (mockup rule). (2) The order to the phone: finish the real-data import (money, Quran, prayer and the rest), try and check every room with it, check everything is fine, then install on the owner's iPhone for daily use. His own use is expected to surface most problems.

Owner, 23 Oct: cloud. «انا رابط التطبيق الاول بفاير بيس وكلاود ابي نفس الشي». The owner wants his data in the cloud as in the old app (Firebase + cloud), not on the phone alone. This lifts RA1 (local-only v1) and the sync freeze. Answered 24 Oct: the owner chose iCloud / CloudKit. Implement the private database after the local import trial. The data model is already CloudKit-shaped; it is free and in the owner's own iCloud; no third-party SDK; it needs the paid developer account. With Firebase, diary and money must be end-to-end encrypted before upload, and the SDK licence and size apply. Before daily use on the phone, there must at least be an automatic encrypted backup off the device.

Answered by the owner, 21 Oct (details in DECISIONS):
1. PDF to books: build both parts — (a) through Turath (find the same book there and import it), (b) on-device conversion of any PDF the owner adds.
2. Encrypted backup: unfrozen — build it («سوها»). App-switcher privacy, sync and the contrast inspector stay frozen.
3. Quran day (item 13): agent does one final check; the owner tries it later in the full app trial.
4. Mihbara v2 desks: unchanged (v2 under RA8).
5. iOS 27 simulator and enlarged-text runs: never; do not download, test or plan them.

Answered by the owner, 18 Oct (details in DECISIONS):
1. Turath: personal reading is fine; the books are waqf for Muslims. New request: nice quoting from Turath books (a quote card with book, author, volume/page and source).
2. Literata: yes, bundle it with its OFL file.
3. M6-V11 focus reader and M6-V22 auto-scroll are v1; build them.
4. M6-V13/V14 English settings and word card: «خل كوديكس يسوي كل شي». Build them fully in v1 (device dictionary, plus Apple Translation behind availability).
5. Zakat (silver nisab, debt options): «ابحث انت عن الموضوع واذا شفته تمام اعتمده». The agent researches recognised fiqh sources, adopts what is sound, and records the sources in DECISIONS.
6. RM5 pairing window: 10 minutes, kept.
7. RM6 accuracy: the owner authorises using the bank messages on this Mac (Messages), from every bank, and keeping them so the parser learns each bank's exact format («عادي تحفظها عندك»). Store the real messages in `Madar/Private/bank-messages/` (git-ignored, on this Mac only). Never store OTP codes. A local test reads them when the folder exists. Fixtures committed to Git are anonymised copies.

Resolved 21 Oct (owner: «سوه بخط ثمانية»): the Thmanyah licence forbids editing the font files, so «٬/٫» are drawn at display time from Thmanyah's own «،» and «,» across the money room (`Text(figure:)`, `displayFigure`). Amounts outside the money room (Quran plan decimals, zakat rate) still use the plain string.

M5 (16 Oct, updated 19 Oct): the owner delegates the Zakat wording/source review to the agent, superseding the wait for an owner-selected specialist. Sources and debt-method distinctions are now in V22; this is not qualified-human certification. RM5 remains 10 minutes; real bank parser accuracy still needs the authorised local corpus evidence.

Owner standing rule (15 Oct): screens that match their design or better count as approved pending the owner's app trial (M3 matched screens included). Owner visually approved M2-V01–V17 and the prayer/Quran link on 15 October. OS gallery/Control invocation, real notification delivery, named human resource release sample, owner-iPhone and milestone gates remain separately unverified/deferred.

- Ending a trip-linked habit pause when a trip is dismissed: should dismissal end the linked `HabitPause` on that Madar day, and what provenance is saved for replay (Ideas row «End a trip-linked habit pause when a trip is dismissed»)? **Owner 27 Oct: the habit resumes on the dismissal day — ready.**

- J05 freeze-boundary field: the diary capture/freeze/margin schema decision (item 27 and the 26 Oct Codex handover) still awaits the owner. **Owner 27 Oct: past days stay as written; a prayer-setting change affects today onward only — ready.**
- Where did «صفحة ٩٤» appear? Row 30e («خلاني كأنني في صفحة ٩٤») needs the owner to say where it showed; no spontaneous jump to page 94 was reproduced on a fresh conversion. **Owner 27 Oct: does not remember / resolved — closed.**

## Ideas — for the owner
- **The «جيد» review interval stops growing (Claude, 29 Oct, Haiku hunt):** in the Quran review scheduler a portion answered «جيد» again and again settles near its first «جيد» gap, because ease decays as it multiplies. A memorised portion that is always good could space out further (for example ×1.3 each time up to a cap). This changes a scheduling rule, so it waits for your yes.
- **Mihbara notebook line on widgets (Claude, 28 Oct, AU-12):** the Mihbara widget can show the title of the notebook you last wrote in, while diary text on widgets waits for an opt-in (`notes.widgetContent`, RD7 row below). Should notebook titles follow the same opt-in? Today they show by default; under the app lock widgets already hide content.
- **City outside the three presets (Claude, 28 Oct, AU-10):** «مدينة أخرى» asks a new user for latitude, longitude, an English time-zone ID, a country and a currency code. A searchable city list or a «استخدم موقعي» button (with one sentence on why) would fill these in. Needs a city dataset or location permission, so it waits for your yes.
- **Settings titles that do not say what they change (Claude, 28 Oct, AU-08):** «ميزانية اليوم» (`notif.dailyBudget`, it is a count of reminders) → «أقصى عدد للتذكيرات في اليوم»; «المتأخر قبل أن يبعد» (`quran.lateReminder`) → «ذكّرني بالورد المتأخر»; «عائلة ودجات الآيفون» (`bahw.iphoneWidgetFamily`, the options are styles) → «نمط ودجات الآيفون»; «نشاط حيّ للصلاة القادمة» → «الصلاة القادمة على شاشة القفل». These are schema titles, so they wait for your yes.

**Owner, 18 Oct: every idea below is approved («كلها»).** Build them as additive, optional fields or settings, with defaults, a reset and undo. Test any migration on synthetic stores only, with one review pass (store-migration exception). Order: finish the M6 cards first, then these ideas inside their rooms, then M7. M7 starts straight away when they are done; there is no wait for an owner trial. New idea, also approved: a Mihbara quote card as an image (text, book, author, volume/page, source) to save or share. Turath quotes carry the source link.

Bigger improvement ideas from Claude or Codex (new features, rule or data-model changes, beyond v1). Not built until the owner says yes. One row per idea.

Needed to faithfully migrate one source event; its whole raw row is currently archived, with no native countdown silently losing the requested behavior. Requires additive schema, date math and migration/replay tests before installation. | Owner-data trial, 25 Oct | Approved by owner, 25 Oct: «نعم، أضف الخيار». Built 25 Oct (Claude): V11, editor toggle, lobby/year/widget «مضى …», import mapping; see DECISIONS. |

| On-device PDF-to-EPUB conversion (Mihbara) | Resolve with Claude as a separate end-to-end topic: local Swift flow; inspect PDFKit Arabic extraction/order without blindly reversing text; test Vision Arabic OCR for scanned pages; create a reflowable EPUB with `lang="ar"`, `dir="rtl"`, and OPF `page-progression-direction="rtl"`; review OFL font and ZIPFoundation (MIT) licensing; validate samples with EPUBCheck in development. Claude skill references: `claude-code-apple-skills`, `xcode27-skills`, Axiom. `pdf-to-epub`, Marker, and `pdf2text-arabic` are prototype/reference tools only, not app dependencies. Arabic extraction/OCR accuracy and implementation scope remain open; outside current v1 import/reader cards. **Claude scope proposal, 21 Oct (awaiting owner):** everything needed already exists locally: `PDFBookTextReader` (embedded text first, Vision OCR for image pages, malformed-mapping detection) and the Turath EPUB writer (`TurathBook.build`/`encode`, own ZIP + CRC, `lang="ar"`, RTL spine), so no new dependency or licence. *v1 (recommended):* one action on an imported PDF, «حوّله إلى كتاب منساب», builds a new EPUB beside the original: one section per printed page with a page anchor (quotes keep the page citation), OCR pages marked, pages whose embedded Arabic is malformed shown as a link back to the original page instead of garbled text; the PDF and its records stay unchanged; cancelable progress; additive only. *v2:* headings/TOC from the PDF outline, footnote separation, tables, EPUBCheck in development. | Owner research and direction, 18 Oct | Answered 21 Oct: both parts (Turath + on-device). Built as M6-T02; EPUBCheck, footnotes and tables stay open. |
| Honorifics in scanned books | In the owner's «مشروع العمر» the calligraphic ﷺ / رضي الله عنه / رحمه الله glyphs OCR as noise words («وَلِيو»، «صينه»، «زجْم لته»). Option: after «رسول الله»/«النبي» or a Companion's name, replace a short non-word with the honorific (or drop it). It adds words the scan did not read, so it waits for the owner. | Claude, 26 Oct | open |
| Delete a user’s CloudKit copy | Add a clear, confirmed way to remove this user’s synced records and media from the private iCloud database while retaining or separately managing the device copy. Turning sync off currently stops future mirroring but leaves the existing cloud copy. Apple documents that SwiftData sync uses `NSPersistentCloudKitContainer`, and Core Data provides `purgeObjectsAndRecordsInZone…` to remove server records with corresponding managed objects; direct `CKDatabase.deleteRecordZone` is permanent. The current Madar sources use SwiftData and expose no purge bridge, so a safe removal path must prove deletion does not re-upload records or remove the device copy, then be tested in a signed development container with explicit user confirmation. [SwiftData sync](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftdata/syncing-model-data-across-a-persons-devices) · [Apple purge API](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/coredata/nspersistentcloudkitcontainer/purgeobjectsandrecordsinzonewithid:inpersistentstore:completion:) · [Apple zone deletion](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/cloudkit/ckdatabase/deleterecordzone%28withid%3A%29). | Codex privacy/distribution audit, 26 Oct | open |
| End a trip-linked habit pause when a trip is dismissed | A restored or synced `Trip` with `dismissed == true` and no end date can leave a linked open `HabitPause` frozen indefinitely: `HabitRepository` only derives the pause end from `Trip.end`. The current travel question stores «لا، أنا مقيم هنا» separately, so this state is not produced by the current UI. Before legacy/restored/synced dismissed trips can reach users, decide whether dismissal ends the pause on that Madar day and what provenance must be saved for deterministic replay/history; this changes behavior/data and waits for owner direction. | Codex journey scan, 26 Oct | open |
| Money amounts in diary export | Diary auto lines «صرفت ٤٥ ر.س في …» (kind `mal`, default on) go into the PDF/Markdown export as-is, even when `core.hideMoneyOutside` (default on: «أخفِ المال خارج التطبيق») or `core.hideMoneyUntilTouch` is set. An export leaves the app, so the owner should choose: keep amounts (the user asked for the export), drop the amount and keep «صرفت في …», or ask in the export sheet. Inside the app the diary and lobby are not «outside», so they stay as they are. | Claude audit, 26 Oct | open |

## M5 Money — owner opened 16 October («وابدا بالمال انت»), engine first at full strength

Read before any card: §7.5 `sections/12-r-mal.md` end to end (numbers table, engine constants, L01–L14, F01–F11), §11 money models `17-data.md`, and RM1–RM10 in the fixes file (they override §7.5). Engines are pure Swift in `Packages/MadarMoney` (value types that mirror the dictionary fields, never SwiftData, never the name `Transaction`, RA4); every expected value comes from the spec, with hand arithmetic in the test. Every amount is `Int` minor units; every number has one definition in `MoneyEngine` (L09). Screens: canonical `docs/HANDOFF/screens/mal/NN-*.jpg`. Do not copy on any money screen (RM10, RL10): red for overspent days (clay `due` only, and only for debt envelopes), amounts on the lock screen or in notifications while `core.hideMoneyOutside`, receivables, a 78/100 score, an essential/luxury split, the travel button on home, «؟», «دخلت وحدها», «إشارات المال», dialect copy («يمديك»، «تجاهل هالمرة» → فصحى قريبة, RU4), sample numbers and names (يومية ٤٠٠، البلاتينيوم ٤٨٢١، هدية أمي…). Nil fields keep the old monthly `dueDay` rows. Pure `Recurrence` in MadarCore: every N days/weeks/months/years from the first day, month/year steps counted from the first day and clamped to the month end (31 Jan → 28/29 Feb → 31 Mar), never chained; occurrences in a range, next after a day, the next three, Arabic phrases («كل ٦ أشهر»، «بعد ٤ أشهر ويومين»); a linked payment settles the nearest due day. One reading (`ObligationSchedule`) feeds the salary opening, the money home and «الالتزامات». `money.saveObligation` accepts the interval and first day (bill/subscription; loans/instalments keep monthly), undo kept. Add sheet: kind فاتورة/اشتراك/قرض/تقسيط, name, amount, «يتكرر كل N [يوم/أسبوع/شهر/سنة]», first-day picker with its distance, the next three due days in both calendars (primary per core.firstCalendar), effect on the ladder; «تم» above the decimal pad and scroll-to-dismiss (the pad had no way to close). «الاشتراكات» shelf in the obligations screen. The stamp sheet takes an optional reason, shown as «لأن: …» on the receipt and the row. | M6-V06 | Arabic file reader — `06-R9-Qari-Turath.jpg` | matched — awaiting owner | Fixed (Claude, 18 Oct): two real reader bugs. (1) Readium `fontSize` is a multiplier and the reader passed a percentage (100 → 10000%). (2) Madar's book CSP allowed styles and fonts only from the book origin, so Readium's own `readium://assets` CSS (default/after) and fonts were blocked, and size, font, alignment and theme never applied (this also kept the 10000% from taking effect). CSP now adds `readium://assets` to style-src/font-src only; scripts stay `'none'` (Core test pins it). Amiri (OFL, bundled; schema default `font: amiri`) is the face; size 4 = 125% to match the canonical text size; line height 1.95; heading in Amiri bold. The body now shows in the test's own screenshot 3/3 (`M6-V06-x3-*.xcresult`). Codex's earlier blank state was not isolated: the blank frames seen this session were the last frames of recordings from runs that failed before their screenshot step, while DOM probes found the text in place. Printed page now resolves from the visible page-break anchor (Readium locators carry no fragment): «ص ٤٥٣ من ٤٥٤ في المطبوع». The anchor is kept for the label only; the saved locator stays Readium's own, so reopening returns to the exact spot. Verified: `testLocalEPUBOpensInArabicReaderAndClosesBackToItsShelf` 3/3 iterations, Mihbara Home+Import UI 12/12 (with the wird test update), MihbaraReadingTests 16/16. Shot `Artifacts/M6-V06-shots/M6-V06-reader-L.png`, compare `R9-Qari-Turath.html`. Differences: fixture text not the canonical sample; «فصل» without chapter number (fixture has none); no paragraph numbers (not in the fixture); progress bar thinner; footer left says «تُسجَّل الجلسة بعد دقيقة نشاط». RTL multi-page anchor direction verified only on a one-page chapter. 26 Oct follow-on: pending reader session and locator writes retry when store maintenance ends; a recovered write clears only its own stale error. `MihbaraHomeIntegrationTests/testReadingSavesRetryAfterStoreMaintenanceEnds` 1/1 on iPhone 17 Pro/iOS 26.5 (`codex-mihbara-maintenance-retry-r4.xcresult`). No visual or data-model change. |

## Frozen — do not work on until the owner unfreezes

M0-T06 (device acceptance paperwork), M0-T09 privacy matrix (encrypted backup unfrozen by the owner 21 Oct), M0-T12 (contrast inspector), app-switcher privacy, sync. The owner explicitly reopened only the app-level Face ID gate in UX-01; all other frozen areas remain untouched. Earlier evidence: `docs/archive/reports/`, `docs/archive/reviews/`.

# Later

M0-M6 software is integrated; M5 has its bounded software gate. M7 remains open for every-screen visual/state trace and owner/device/system acceptance (owner/Claude), real-device Release performance, iOS 27 runtime, resource release evidence and signed distribution. PDF OCR/search is now local and cancelable; PDF-to-EPUB conversion remains the separate owner/Claude topic. Direct @als is optional/deferred; no provider key/endpoint is available. Store metadata/privacy text is prepared locally; public support/privacy URLs, real review contact and App Store Connect access are still required for publication. The static scanner remains conservative: its UI/font/colour/action references were not bulk-accepted or turned into a fake complete pass; frozen backup/privacy/a11y work remains frozen.

# Handovers
## Handover — مراجعة ربط مدار والأزرار AU-95 — 2026-10-30 — Codex
- **Works now:** `.madarPlain` يلتقط النقر على مساحة الزر كاملة؛ تأكيدات إغلاق مسودة التفريغ الصوتي وفهرس المحبرة والمصروف تحفظ المدخل أو تتجاهله صراحة؛ التقاط المصروف ينتظر التأكيد ويرتب الطلبات FIFO؛ وجهات المال والتنبيهات المقصودة تعمل؛ جذرا الصلاة والمحبرة يعيدان إلى الشاشة الأصلية بمسار الإغلاق المعتاد.
- **Verified:** `r90` اختبارات الفهرس الفارغ/التراجع/الحفظ والمصروف/الحفظ والتراجع 5/5؛ `r91` اختبار تكامل Capture FIFO ومنع التكرار 1/1؛ `r86` تفريغ غير محفوظ 1/1، `r87` الحفظ والتشغيل 1/1، `r88` تأكيد تراجع فهرس المحبرة 1/1، `r89` حفظ الفهرس والتراجع عنه 2/2. `r67` جذر الصلاة 1/1 و`r62` جذر المحبرة 1/1؛ `git diff --check` نظيف.
- **Differences left:** لم تُطابق هذه الجولة كل الشاشات مع canonical. فُحصت لقطة تأكيد المصروف فقط؛ لا مرجع canonical لحالة الحوار. لا قبول مالك.
- **Not verified:** الزحف الكامل للبهو، FIFO UI المتتابع، iPhone المالك. محاولات `r81–r84` فيها fixture/session errors ولا تُحسب نجاحًا. `notes/write` الثاني قد يزيل نص المحرر قبل debounce، و`madar://bahw` قد يزيل محرر العادة غير المحفوظ؛ هذان تحليلان ساكنان لم يُعاد إنتاجهما على UI. Quran/diary/money roots لا تعيد من شاشة فرعية، وجذر Mihbara لا يغلق كل الأوراق؛ رجوع Quran Drill أثناء الانشغال يتجاهل الضغط.
- **Next:** احمِ محرر اليوميات والعادة من استبدال الرابط لهما، واختبر ذلك على UI؛ عالج توجيه الجذور وحراس الخروج الأخرى، ثم أعد زحف البهو. لا تغيّر بيانات المالك.
- **Model/effort:** غير مكشوف / غير مكشوف؛ مساعدو البحث للقراءة فقط.
- **Usage:** آخر قراءة قبل الحفظ: ٢١٪ من نافذة الخمس ساعات و٩٣٪ من النافذة الأسبوعية مستهلكة (٧٪ متبقية)؛ أعد القياس بعد الالتزام.
